- **goal**: Clarify and validate a development goal before writing a PRD. Use when the user has an idea or intent but hasn't shaped it into requirements yet — phrases like 'I want to build X', 'I'm thinking about adding Y', 'we should improve Z', 'what should we work on next', 'help me think through this idea'.
